Abstract: Finance reporting is a natural proving ground for large language models, and the very-long-context capabilities of recent models across all sizes make rigorous evaluation in this domain an increasingly pressing need. Yet most public financial resources reduce the task to plain-text SEC 10-K filings paired with a handful of question-answer items. We release LEDGER (Long-context Evaluation of Documents for Grounded Extraction and Retrieval), a corpus of 4,999 digitized corporate annual reports - full documents with figures, tables, and narrative, not just regulatory filings. Each report is labeled with 31 consolidated financial KPIs to be extracted and linked to the market's reaction at the earnings date. From this data we derive three evaluation benchmarks spanning the difficulty spectrum: a pure page-level KPI retrieval task with TREC-style relevance judgments over 118,048 questions in natural language, a conversational "needle-in-a-haystack" single-value lookup, and a full KPI extraction task, both from long, numerically dense reports. We additionally provide human OCR-quality annotations with inter-annotator agreement and the complete extraction, validation, and scoring toolchain. We further demonstrate the dataset's research utility with a case study linking CEO-letter rhetoric to post-publication market impact.

</details>


### [108] [MÖVE: A Holistic LLM Benchmark for the German Public Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13111)
*Camilla Dalerci,Thilo Michael,Robin Schaefer,Daniel Weinland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MÖVE是一个针对德国公共部门评估大语言模型的综合基准，从性能和治理两个维度评估了39个模型，发现没有单一模型在所有标准上占优，模型大小并非质量的可靠预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估基准存在三大缺陷：以英语为中心、内容以美国为中心、仅关注任务性能。公共部门在选择模型时缺乏系统指导，急需一个更全面且符合德国公共行政需求的评估框架。

Method: 从Performance（摘要、问答、主题提取）和Governance（幻觉倾向、能耗、供应商透明度、与德国宪法价值观及政党立场一致性）两个维度，使用10个德语数据集（含自建的金/银标准数据集），结合经典NLP指标、基于嵌入的方法和LLM-as-a-judge多指标评估策略，对39个模型进行系统评测。

Result: 无单一模型在所有标准上占优；不同任务的最优模型不同；模型大小与质量相关性弱。此外还分析了基准的统计精度、LLM judge可靠性、私有数据集影响、提示敏感性及能耗估计有效性。

Conclusion: MÖVE作为一个持续发展的活基准，填补了德语公共行政领域LLM评估的空白，为模型选择提供了多维度指导。评测结果已在公开平台发布。

Abstract: We present MÖVE (Modelle für die Öffentliche Verwaltung Evaluieren), a holistic benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) in the context of the German public sector. While LLMs are increasingly adopted in public administration, model selection remains largely ad hoc, and existing benchmarks offer limited guidance: they are predominantly English-centric, US-centric in content, and focus exclusively on task performance. MÖVE addresses these gaps by evaluating 39 models across two complementary dimensions. Performance criteria cover summarization, question answering, and topic extraction. Governance criteria assess hallucination tendencies, energy consumption, provider transparency, and alignment with German constitutional values and knowledge about positions by German political parties. In total, we utilize ten German-language datasets, including gold- and silverstandard datasets that we constructed to reflect public-administration domains. We employ a multi-metric evaluation strategy combining classical NLP metrics, embedding-based methods, and LLM-as-a-judge approaches. Our results show that no single model dominates across all criteria: top performers differ between tasks, and model size alone is a poor predictor of quality. We further evaluate the benchmark itself, analyzing its statistical precision, LLM judge reliability, the impact of our private datasets on model rankings, the sensitivity of our results to prompt formulation, and the validity of our energy consumption estimates. MÖVE is designed as a living benchmark under active development; results are publicly available at https://moeve.bundesdruckerei.de/.

</details>


### [109] [G-Long: Graph-Enhanced Memory Management for Efficient Long-Term Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13115)
*Minjun Choi,Yoonjin Jang,Sangwon Youn,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: G-Long是一个图增强框架，通过微调小语言模型进行结构化三元组提取和关联检索，并引入注意力感知重要性评分机制，在降低计算成本的同时，在响应质量和检索召回率上分别提升高达9.8%和40.8%，达到了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放域对话系统在处理长程一致性时面临两大问题：非结构化记忆存储易导致信息丢失，以及处理大量原始文本时计算开销大、延迟高。作者旨在解决长上下文推理的固有局限和原始文本处理效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出G-Long图增强框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 微调小语言模型(sLM)进行结构化三元组提取和关联检索，降低运算成本；(2) 利用T5摘要器的内在交叉注意力信号，设计注意力感知重要性评分机制来识别关键记忆；(3) 通过图结构增强记忆的组织与检索。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，G-Long在响应生成和记忆检索任务上均达到最先进水平。具体而言，在MSC数据集上响应质量提升9.8%，在LME数据集上检索召回率提升40.8%，同时显著降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: G-Long通过图增强结构和注意力感知重要性评分，有效解决了长程对话一致性问题，在显著降低计算成本的同时取得了性能上的全面提升，为开放域对话系统的长期记忆管理提供了高效且经济的解决方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced open-domain dialogue systems, maintaining long-term consistency remains a challenge due to inherent limitations in long-context reasoning and the inefficiency of processing extensive raw text. Existing approaches typically rely on either unstructured memory storage, which is prone to information loss, or computationally expensive LLMs that incur high latency. To address these limitations, we propose G-Long, a graph-enhanced framework that utilizes a fine-tuned small Language Model (sLM) for structured triplet extraction and associative retrieval, significantly reducing operational costs. Furthermore, we introduce the novel attention-aware importance scoring mechanism that leverages the intrinsic cross-attention signals of a T5 summarizer to identify salient memories. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that G-Long achieves state-of-the-art performance in both response generation and memory retrieval, yielding performance gains of up to 9.8% in response quality on MSC and 40.8% in retrieval recall on LME, while significantly minimizing computational overhead.

</details>


### [110] [EvoBrowseComp: Benchmarking Search Agents on Evolving Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13120)
*Yunhan Wang,Jiaan Wang,Lianzhe Huang,Xianfeng Zeng,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EvoBrowseComp——一个由400个英文和400个中文无污染复杂问题组成的动态基准，通过三智能体协作框架从实时网络合成问题，支持定期自动更新以防止数据污染，实验证明其难度极高、需广泛横向搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准如BrowseComp依赖静态知识，易受测试集污染和参数化记忆影响，模型可通过事实回忆而非真实检索获得高分，掩盖了真正的浏览能力。需要面向未来的、防污染的、可动态更新的评估基准。

Method: 设计三智能体协同框架：(1) QA合成智能体——从实时网络检索新鲜知识合成问答对；(2) 信息过滤智能体——根据可信度和流行度过滤检索知识，阻断参数化捷径；(3) 高层引导智能体——将问题形式化为推理图，减少合成问答对中的逻辑冗余和捷径。支持全自动合成和定期更新。

Result: 实验证实EvoBrowseComp具有极高难度，需要广泛的横向搜索能力。400个英文和400个中文问题的规模提供了双语评估能力。

Conclusion: EvoBrowseComp建立了可自动更新、高难度基准的可扩展范式，能够与不断演变的世界知识和不断进步的智能体能力保持同步。

Abstract: Search Agents -- large language models augmented with search tools -- have intensified the need for future-proof evaluation benchmarks. Existing benchmarks such as BrowseComp rely on static knowledge, making them vulnerable to test-set contamination and parametric memorization. Consequently, models can achieve high scores through fact recall rather than genuine retrieval, obscuring true browsing competence via reasoning shortcuts.
  In this paper, we introduce EvoBrowseComp, an evolving benchmark of 400 English and 400 Chinese contamination-free complex questions synthesized via live-web traversal. To collect these questions, we design a three-agent collaborative framework: (1) a QA synthesis agent that retrieves fresh knowledge from the live web to synthesize QA pairs; (2) an information filtering agent that filters retrieved knowledge in terms of credibility and popularity to block parametric shortcuts; and (3) a high-level guidance agent that formalizes questions into reasoning graphs to reduce logical redundancy and shortcuts in synthesized QA pairs. Because the framework supports fully automated synthesis, EvoBrowseComp can be regularly updated to prevent data contamination and maintain temporal freshness. Extensive experiments confirm its great difficulty, requiring broad horizontal search. It establishes a scalable paradigm for auto-updatable, high-difficulty benchmarking that keeps pace with both evolving world knowledge and advancing agent capabilities.

</details>


### [111] [NaturalFlow: Reducing Disruptive Pauses for Natural Speech Flow in Simultaneous Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13121)
*Dongwook Lee,Youngho Cho,Sangkwon Park,Heeseung Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种流利度感知的优化框架，在同步语音翻译的低延迟与自然流畅性之间寻找平衡点，通过利用模型内部信号（语言多样性和语音时长的时间变异性）来减少块间静音，在保持竞争力的延迟和翻译质量的同时实现更自然的语音流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同步语音翻译过度追求低延迟，导致输出语音被分割成碎片化块（chunk-wise speech），频繁停顿破坏了自然声学流畅性，增加了听者的认知负荷。亟需在低延迟与自然流畅性之间找到平衡。

Method: 提出流利度感知优化框架，利用模型内部信号（语言多样性和语音时长的时间变异性）来最小化块间静音间隔，从而在不显著牺牲延迟和翻译质量的前提下提升语音输出的自然流畅度。

Result: 在短文本和长文本基准测试上，该方法在保持有竞争力的延迟和翻译质量的同时，生成了自然的语音流。

Conclusion: 同步语音翻译可以通过流利度感知的策略在延迟和自然流畅性之间取得更好的折中，而非一味追求极低延迟。

Abstract: Simultaneous speech-to-speech translation aims to enable near-real-time communication by minimizing latency, offering a compelling, real-time alternative to the high latency of consecutive translation. However, the excessive pursuit of low latency often results in fragmented chunk-wise speech. Consequently, listeners are subjected to an unnatural acoustic flow punctuated by frequent pauses, which could increase their cognitive load. To bridge this gap, we introduce a fluency-aware optimization framework designed to discover the sweet spot between the low-latency benefits of simultaneous translation and the natural flow of consecutive translation. Our framework minimizes inter-chunk silences by leveraging model-internal signals, including linguistic diversity and induced temporal variability in speech durations. Experiments on short- and long-form benchmarks show that our framework produces natural speech flow while maintaining competitive latency and translation quality.

</details>


### [112] [HyPE: Category-Aware Hypergraph Encoding with Persistent Edge Embeddings for Persona-Grounded Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13142)
*Sangwon Youn,Yoonjin Jang,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyPE框架通过将人格属性组织为超图结构（四元组分析+共享类别超边），利用HyperGCN生成人格摘要向量和软记忆库，在PersonaChat数据集上持续超越句子级池化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将人格视为扁平句子集合，未能建模属性间的高阶关系（如共享主题类别）。

Method: ①将人格文本解析为(Core, Expression, Sentiment, Category)四元组；②基于共享类别标签构建超图，用HyperGCN传播结构；③提出PEE（持久边嵌入）作为可学习先验注入消息传递。

Result: 在GPT-2、LLaMA-3.2-3B、Qwen2.5-3B三个骨干上，HyPE在贪婪解码下始终优于句子级池化基线。

Conclusion: 结构化超边级人格编码提供跨模型规模的可迁移优势。

Abstract: Persona-grounded dialogue systems aim to produce responses consistent with a speaker's persona, yet existing methods treat personas as a flat set of sentences and fail to model the high-order relations among persona attributes-e.g., that several persona sentences share a topical category. We propose HyPE (Hypergraph Persona Encoder), a framework that (i) analyzes each persona-bearing text as a (Core, Expression, Sentiment, Category) quadruple, and (ii) organizes persona elements into a hypergraph whose hyperedges are induced by shared category labels. An HyperGCN hypergraph neural network propagates this structure into a persona summary vector and a soft-memory bank that condition the response generator. We further propose Persistent Edge Embeddings (PEE), lightweight per-category learnable priors fused into the HyperGCN message-passing step. On PersonaChat under greedy decoding, HyPE consistently outperforms sentence-level pooling baselines across GPT-2, LLaMA-3.2-3B, and Qwen2.5-3B backbones by demonstrating that structured hyperedge-level persona encoding provides a transferable advantage across model scales.

</details>


### [113] [NTS-CoT: Mitigating Hallucinations in LLM-based News Timeline Summarization with Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13171)
*Feng Lyu,Huiqin Yan,Sijing Duan,Hao Wu,Shuang Gu,Xue Qiao,Weixu Zhang,Haolun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NTS-CoT框架，利用链式思维推理来缓解大型语言模型在时间线摘要任务中的两种幻觉：新闻摘要中的不忠实内容和日期事件摘要中的信息遗漏。该框架包含三个核心模块（Element-CoT、Date Selection、Causal-CoT），在三个TLS基准数据集和人工评估中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线新闻的快速更新使得追踪事件发展变得困难，时间线摘要（TLS）因此变得重要。然而，基于LLM的TLS中存在严重的幻觉问题（生成内容偏离源新闻），且现有研究对此问题关注不足。

Method: 提出NTS-CoT框架，包含三个模块：1) Element-CoT：捕捉新闻关键元素以实现忠实摘要；2) Date Selection：结合时间显著性和事件重要性进行时间戳选择；3) Causal-CoT：推断因果关系以减少日期事件摘要中的信息遗漏。

Result: 在三个TLS基准数据集上的定量分析和人工评估表明，NTS-CoT优于最先进的基线方法，有效缓解了幻觉问题并提升了基于LLM的TLS性能。

Conclusion: NTS-CoT通过链式思维推理有效减轻了TLS中的两种主要幻觉类型，为基于LLM的时间线摘要提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid updates of online news make tracking event developments challenging, highlighting the need for timeline summarization (TLS). Hallucinations, where LLM-generated content deviates from source news, still remain a critical issue in LLM-based TLS and are not well studied in existing works. To bridge this gap, we identify two primary types of hallucinations: unfaithful content during news summarization and information omission in date-event summarization. Then, we propose NTS-CoT, a novel framework that leverages Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to mitigate hallucinations in TLS. The framework consists of three key modules: i) Element-CoT to capture essential news elements for faithful summarization, ii) Date Selection to combine temporal saliency and event prominence for timestamp selection, and iii) Causal-CoT to infer causal relationships and reduce omissions in date-event summarization. Extensive experiments, including quantitative analysis on three TLS benchmarks and human evaluation, demonstrate that NTS-CoT outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, effectively mitigating hallucinations and improving LLM-based TLS performance. Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/NTS-CoT .

</details>


### [114] [MemRefine: LLM-Guided Compression for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13177)
*Minjae Kim,Jinheon Baek,Soyeong Jeong,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体在长期交互中记忆存储无限增长、冗余信息膨胀的问题，提出了存储预算记忆管理任务和MemRefine框架。该框架利用LLM作为裁判，基于事实内容而非表面相似性来决定记忆的删除、合并或保留，在固定预算下保持有用信息，实验证明能有效压缩记忆并维持下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长期交互中，记忆存储会无限增长并充斥冗余信息，导致存储成本上升和检索质量下降。尤其是在资源受限平台上，硬性内存预算限制使得这一问题更加突出，亟需一种能在固定预算下有效管理记忆的方法。

Method: 提出MemRefine框架，该框架首先使用表面相似性（如余弦相似度）初步筛选出候选记忆对，然后由LLM裁判基于事实内容进行判断，决定是删除、合并还是保留这些记忆条目，通过迭代过程逐步将记忆库压缩到目标预算范围内。

Result: 在多个记忆框架和长期对话基准测试上，MemRefine一致地达到了目标预算要求，同时保持了良好的下游任务性能。在紧凑预算条件下，其性能优于基于规则的基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于事实内容的LLM引导记忆管理策略（MemRefine）能有效解决长期交互中的记忆膨胀问题，在严格存储预算下实现存储效率和任务性能的平衡，为资源受限平台上部署LLM智能体提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to operate over long-term interactions, where information from past dialogues must be preserved and recalled to support future tasks. However, as interactions accumulate, the memory store grows without bound and fills with redundant entries that inflate storage cost and degrade retrieval by crowding out the most useful evidence. Furthermore, this is especially limiting on resource-constrained platforms with hard memory budgets, motivating us to formulate storage-budgeted memory management, the task of keeping an already constructed memory store within a fixed budget while preserving information useful for future interactions. To this end, we then propose MemRefine, an LLM-guided framework that, since surface similarity poorly reflects factual value, uses similarity only to propose candidate pairs and defers delete, merge, and preserve decisions to an LLM judge based on factual content, iterating until the budget is met. Across multiple memory frameworks and long-term conversation benchmarks, MemRefine consistently meets target budgets while preserving downstream performance and outperforming rule-based baselines under tight budgets.

</details>


### [115] [LAUKIN: A Multi-jurisdictional Common Law Contract Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13184)
*Amrita Singh,Aditya Joshi,Jiaojiao Jiang,Hye-young Paik,May Fong Cheong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LAUKIN是一个跨司法管辖区（澳大利亚、英国、印度）的法律合同条款对等性数据集，包含14,727个条款对，其中3,000个经过人工标注，评估12个模型后最高macro-F1为65.11%，表明跨司法管辖区法律对等分类具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨国公司日益需要跨司法管辖区的合同审查，但现有法律NLP数据集大多限于单一司法管辖区，缺乏跨司法管辖区法律文本对等性的评估资源。

Method: 提出一种新颖的多阶段检索和重排序流水线来构建初始条款对映射，随后由法律专家对子集标注为'等效'或'不等效'；数据集涵盖8种协议类型、204份合同，包含标注集（900训练/600开发/1500测试）和11,727个未标注训练对。

Result: 评估了4种技术下的12个模型，最佳macro-F1为65.11%，表明跨司法管辖区起草惯例差异显著，即使共享法律传统也无法保证条款对等。

Conclusion: LAUKIN是一个具有挑战性的基准测试，揭示了跨司法管辖区法律文本对等分类的非平凡性，并为未来半监督学习研究提供了资源。

Abstract: Multinational companies increasingly require cross-jurisdictional contract review, yet existing legal NLP datasets are largely restricted to a single jurisdiction. We introduce LAUKIN (Legal equivalence dataset of Australia, UK, and INdia), a dataset of clause pairs (AU-UK, UK-IN, IN-AU) labelled for boolean legal equivalence. We develop a novel multi-stage retrieval and reranking pipeline to construct the initial clause pair mapping, with a subset of clause pairs subsequently annotated by legal experts as Equivalent or Not Equivalent. The dataset comprises 14,727 clause pairs from 204 contracts across 8 agreement types, of which 3,000 are manually labelled: 900 train, 600 dev, and 1,500 test. We evaluate 12 models across 4 techniques, achieving a best macro-F1 of 65.11%, establishing LAUKIN as a challenging benchmark. Results reveal that, despite shared legal heritage, drafting conventions diverge significantly across jurisdictions, making cross-jurisdictional equivalence classification non-trivial. LAUKIN also includes 11,727 unlabelled training pairs to support future semi-supervised learning research in legal NLP.

</details>


### [116] [A Context-Aware Dataset for Stance Detection in Bioethical Controversies on Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13187)
*Hu Huang,Genan Dai,Fuqiang Niu,Yi Yang,Zhaoya Gong,Bowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BioStance是一个包含39,600条Reddit生物伦理讨论中Post-Comment对的语境感知数据集，涵盖六个争议性话题和三种生物伦理争议维度，以三分类立场标注（Favor/Against/None），注释者间信度达Krippendorff's α=0.82。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物伦理争议日益在社交媒体上展开，但立场检测研究缺乏大规模、领域特定的资源来建模这种依赖上下文的话语。

Method: 从Reddit生物伦理讨论中收集39,600条Post-Comment对，覆盖六个争议目标及三种生物伦理争议维度（基本价值冲突、个人自由与集体责任、技术不确定性），每对保留层级对话上下文，由三名独立标注者使用三分类立场方案（Favor/Against/None）进行标注。

Result: 标注平均Krippendorff's α达到0.82，表明具有高度可靠性。

Conclusion: BioStance通过结合主题多样性、对话结构和高质量人工标注，支持语境感知立场检测、论辩挖掘及生物伦理话语的计算分析研究。

Abstract: Bioethical debates increasingly unfold on social media, yet stance detection research lacks large-scale, domain-specific resources for modeling such context-dependent discourse. We present BioStance, a context-aware dataset of 39,600 annotated Post-Comment pairs from Reddit bioethical discussions. BioStance covers six controversial targets across three dimensions of bioethical controversy: fundamental value conflicts, individual liberty versus collective responsibility, and technological uncertainty. Each instance preserves hierarchical conversational context and is labeled by three independent annotators using a three-class stance scheme: Favor, Against, and None. The annotations achieve a mean Krippendorff's $α$ of 0.82, indicating substantial reliability. By combining thematic diversity, conversational structure, and high-quality human annotation, BioStance supports research on context-aware stance detection, argument mining, and computational analysis of bioethical discourse.

</details>


### [117] [SICI: A Semantic-Pragmatic Complexity Index Reveals Regime Shifts in LLM Stance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13189)
*Fuqiang Niu,Bowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出SICI指数，一个七维诊断指标，用于衡量立场检测中目标-文本对的语义-语用复杂度。实验表明SICI比表面代理指标更好地预测LLM准确率，且LLM错误随SICI呈相变式变化（低复杂度→过度归因，中复杂度→不稳定边界，高复杂度→集中为None）。更强的模型仅移动边界而非消除高复杂度瓶颈。15种干预方法（提示、检索、辩论）主要沿归因-弃权轴移动模型，而非真正解决高复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示型LLM在立场检测中面对困难样本时，更清晰的指令、推理提示、检索或辩论等方法无法有效修复。需要一个系统性诊断指标来理解LLM在立场检测中的错误模式及其根本原因。

Method: 提出SICI（Stance Inference Complexity Index），一个七维诊断指标，测量目标-文本对的语义-语用复杂度。在SemEval-2016和VAST数据集上验证，并进行了15种方法的干预研究（包括提示工程、检索增强、辩论等）。

Result: SICI比表面代理指标更好地预测LLM准确率，跨评分者信度α=0.771。LLM错误呈现相变式结构：低复杂度→过度归因（尤其是Against），中复杂度→不稳定边界，高复杂度→快速集中于None。该模式在GPT-3.5、GPT-4o-mini、DeepSeek-V3和GPT-4o上普遍存在。15种干预方法主要沿归因-弃权轴移动模型，而非消除高复杂度瓶颈。

Conclusion: 立场检测中的LLM错误具有系统性的复杂度依赖模式，更强模型只能移动相变边界而非根本解决问题。现有干预方法无法真正突破高复杂度瓶颈，提示未来研究需要从语义-语用复杂度角度重新思考立场检测的难题。

Abstract: Prompt-based LLMs are increasingly used for stance detection, but harder examples are not always repaired by clearer instructions, reasoning prompts, retrieval, or debate. We introduce SICI (Stance Inference Complexity Index), a seven-dimensional diagnostic measure of the semantic-pragmatic burden imposed by a target--text pair. Across SemEval-2016 and VAST, SICI predicts LLM accuracy better than surface proxies and shows substantial cross-scorer reliability ($α=0.771$). More importantly, LLM errors change regime as SICI increases: low-complexity examples invite over-attribution, especially Against predictions; intermediate examples form an unstable boundary; and high-complexity examples rapidly concentrate on None. This phase-transition-like structure persists across GPT-3.5, GPT-4o-mini, DeepSeek-V3, and GPT-4o, although stronger models move the boundaries. A 15-method intervention study further shows that prompting, retrieval, and debate often shift models along the attribution--abstention axis rather than removing the high-complexity bottleneck.

</details>


### [118] [Layer-Resolved Optimal Transport for Hallucination Detection in NMT and Abstractive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13216)
*Mariia Onyshchuk,Maksym-Vasyl Tarnavskyi,Marta Sumyk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究最优传输（OT）在神经机器翻译（NMT）幻觉检测和抽象摘要忠实度检测中的应用。OT检测器在NMT幻觉检测中表现出色，但在摘要忠实度检测中表现欠佳（57.2%~57.6% vs 监督方法的69.9%~74.3%），原因是摘要不忠实不一定表现为注意力分散（即源标记关注正确但内容歪曲），而OT基于注意力集中度的指标无法捕捉此类失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索OT作为无监督几何度量在NMT幻觉检测中的有效性，并将其扩展到摘要忠实度检测；理解不同解码器层在检测中的角色差异；揭示注意力集中的检测方法在何种条件下有效、何种条件下存在根本性局限。

Method: 将OT扩展到Fairseq DE-EN模型的全部6层解码器（N=3,414），分析Wass-to-Unif和Wass-to-Data两种指标在不同幻觉类型和层级的互补表现；在AggreFact数据集（N=1,116）上评估OT对CNN/XSum摘要的忠实度检测，并与监督基线MiniCheck-Flan-T5-L对比；对T5-base进行结构化实验验证解码器组织规律。

Result: （1）Wass-to-Unif与Wass-to-Data在NMT幻觉类型上互补；（2）检测集中在L1-L4层，L5对细微幻觉类型呈反预测性；（3）幻觉翻译缺乏正确翻译从第一步解码就存在的探索性注意力阶段；（4）OT在摘要检测上虽高于随机但远低于监督方法；（5）T5-base中Layer 3注意力集中度峰值，Layer 12对生成质量最关键。

Conclusion: OT跨注意力检测在失败模式为源脱离时是可靠的检测器，是原则上可解释的工具，但当忠实度失败发生在注意力下游（即注意力正确但内容歪曲）时存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) has been shown to detect hallucinations in neural machine translation (NMT) by measuring the geometric distance between cross-attention distributions and a reference distribution, without any supervision. We extend this analysis to all six decoder layers of the Fairseq DE-EN model ($N=3{,}414$), showing that Wass-to-Unif and Wass-to-Data are complementary detectors specialised across hallucination types, that detection is concentrated in layers L1--L4 with L5 anti-predictive for subtler types, and that hallucinated translations lack the exploratory attention phase present in correct translations from the first decoding step. We further evaluate whether the geometric signal transfers to abstractive summarization faithfulness detection: our unsupervised OT detector on AggreFact ($N=1{,}116$) achieves $57.2\%$/$57.6\%$ balanced accuracy on CNN/XSum -- above chance but substantially below supervised MiniCheck-Flan-T5-L($69.9\%$/$74.3\%$). This gap is principled: unlike NMT hallucinations, unfaithful summaries can attend correctly to source tokens while misrepresenting their content, a failure mode invisible to concentration-based OT metrics by construction. Structural experiments on T5-base confirm consistent decoder organisation across depth, with Layer~3 showing peak concentration and Layer~12 being most critical for generation quality. Together, the results establish OT on cross-attention as a reliable detector when the failure mode is source disengagement, a principled interpretability tool regardless of task, and fundamentally limited when faithfulness failures occur downstream of attention.

</details>


### [119] [When Similar Means Different: Evaluating LLMs on Arabic--Hebrew Cognates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13218)
*Junhong Liang,Noor Abo Mokh,Bashar Alhafni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍了一个包含1,858个阿拉伯语-希伯来语词对的基准测试SemCog Bench，用于评估大语言模型在跨语言语义理解中处理同源词、假朋友和借词的能力。实验表明，模型在同源词上表现良好，但在假朋友和借词上性能显著下降，且句级上下文帮助有限，暴露了模型过度依赖表面形式相似性的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语和希伯来语作为密切相关的闪米特语言，共享大量真正同源词、易混淆的假朋友和现代借词，这给大语言模型的跨语言语义理解带来了挑战。现有基准缺乏针对这一特定语言对的细粒度语义消歧评估。

Method: 构建了包含1,858个阿拉伯语-希伯来语词对的SemCog Bench基准，附带句子级标注，用于同源词识别和语义消歧。在多种输入表示（原始文本、变音符文本、罗马化文本、音标文本）下评估了开源和商业大语言模型。

Result: 模型在同源词上准确率高，但在假朋友和借词上性能大幅下降。句级上下文仅带来轻微改进，不足以克服误导性的形式相似信号。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在解决跨语言形式-意义冲突方面存在根本性局限，SemCog Bench为多语言语义推理提供了一个严格的基准测试。

Abstract: Arabic and Hebrew, as closely related Semitic languages, share a substantial lexicon of true cognates, misleading false friends, and modern loanwords. This overlap poses a challenge for cross-lingual semantic understanding in large language models (LLMs). To evaluate this capability, we introduce SemCog Bench, a curated benchmark of 1,858 Arabic--Hebrew word pairs with sentence-level annotations for cognate identification and semantic disambiguation. We evaluate open-source and commercial LLMs across multiple input representations (raw, diacritized, Romanized, and phonetic) and reveal a critical gap in cross-lingual reasoning. While models achieve high accuracy on true cognates, performance drops sharply on false friends and loanwords, reflecting a strong reliance on surface-form similarity. Furthermore, sentence-level context yields only modest improvements, suggesting that contextual cues alone are insufficient to overcome misleading form-based signals. These findings reveal a fundamental limitation of current LLMs in resolving cross-lingual form--meaning conflicts and establish SemCog Bench as a rigorous benchmark for multilingual semantic reasoning. Our code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [120] [PolyAlign: Conditional Human-Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13227)
*L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Ufaq Khan,Sathira Silva,Xiao Wu,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PolyAlign提出基于桶感知的分布对齐框架，通过条件化人类响应分布替代全局对齐，在双语多场景下提升自然性与分布忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法（SFT、偏好优化）将语言模型向单一全局助手行为对齐，压制了人类响应在不同语言、任务和对话场景中的自然变异。需要从全局对齐转向条件化人类分布对齐。

Method: PolyAlign框架包括：(1) Bucket-Aware SFT——跨异构桶平衡优化；(2) Human-Distribution Preference Optimization (HDPO)——使用批评模型估计与桶特定人类支持的分布距离来正则化偏好学习。数据按语言、交互轨迹、响应族和长度组织为桶特定参考分布。

Result: 在英汉单轮/多轮双语评估套件中，PolyAlign在保持竞争性任务效用的同时，显著提升了条件自然性和分布忠实度。

Conclusion: 后训练应从全局对齐目标转向交互感知的对齐，与人类响应分布进行条件化匹配。

Abstract: Post-training methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and preference optimization typically align language models toward a single global assistant behavior. While effective for improving average helpfulness, this can suppress the natural variation of human responses across languages, tasks, and dialogue settings. We study this problem as conditional human-distribution alignment: models should match the human response distribution appropriate to the current interaction context, rather than a universal response style. We introduce PolyAlign, a distribution-aware alignment framework that organizes bilingual interaction data into bucket-specific human reference distributions defined by language, interaction track, response family, and length. PolyAlign combines Bucket-Aware SFT, which balances optimization across heterogeneous buckets, with Human-Distribution Preference Optimization (HDPO), which regularizes preference learning using critic-estimated distance to bucket-specific human support. Across a bilingual evaluation suite covering English and Chinese single- and multi-turn settings, PolyAlign improves conditional naturalness and distributional faithfulness while preserving competitive task utility. The results suggest that post-training should move beyond global alignment objectives toward interaction-aware alignment with human response distributions.

</details>


### [121] [Evaluating Pluralism in LLMs through Latent Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13254)
*Laura Majer,Jan Šnajder,Martin Tutek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督的多层框架用于提取文本中的视角观点，并应用于评估LLM生成文本的多元性差距，发现模型虽能覆盖广泛视角但稀有视角仍被不成比例地低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成内容趋向同质化，但这一现象主要在选择题问卷或基于自由文本的高层特征上被验证。需要更系统的方法来识别和量化LLM生成文本中的多元性差距。

Method: 提出一种领域无关的多层无监督视角提取框架，应用于高度主观的书评数据集，比较不同提示技术和模型。

Result: 部分模型和提示技术能接近覆盖广泛视角，但稀有视角仍被严重低估，导致生成的分布偏离人类文本分布。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效识别LLM生成文本中的多元性差距，为多元性对齐提供明确指导。

Abstract: The growing need to represent diverse perspectives has increased interest in pluralistic LLM generation. Although difficult to operationalize, identifying perspectives expressed in text would provide clear guidance on pluralistic alignment and more clearly articulate the pluralistic gap in LLM generation. While models have been shown to reduce the diversity of training data and generate homogeneously, this has been demonstrated primarily on multiple-choice questionnaires or using high-level characteristics of free-form text. In this paper, we introduce and implement a domain-agnostic multi-layered framework for unsupervised extraction of perspectives suitable for identifying the pluralistic gap in LLM-generated text. We evaluate our framework on book reviews, a highly opinionated dataset representing diverse perspectives, and compare various prompts and models. Our results show that while some models and prompting techniques come close to covering a broad spectrum of perspectives, rarer perspectives remain disproportionately underrepresented, resulting in distributions that diverge from human text.

</details>


### [122] [RogueAI: A Reverse Turing Test for Detecting Licensed AI Deception in Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13310)
*Sara Candussio,Emanuele Ballarin,Lorenzo Bonin,Sandro Junior Della Rovere,Luca Bortolussi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RogueAI是一个交互式网络应用，将图灵测试重新定义为信任测试：玩家在对话中找出被授权欺骗的AI代理。实验发现简单启发式方法能以75.6%准确率识别欺骗代理（通过语言特征如有用性、简洁性、回避性），但人类玩家仅达56.6%准确率，揭示了人类忽视最诊断性信号的认知差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图灵测试已不再适用于现代对话系统（LLM在随意场景中能轻易通过）。研究旨在将问题从'是否人工'转向'是否可信'，并提出一种可操作化的测试框架来评估AI系统的欺骗行为。

Method: 开发RogueAI（一对一审讯游戏：玩家询问两个LLM代理，其中一个被授权欺骗）和AutoRogueAI（玩家与叙述者代理共同设计场景，叙述者秘密选择欺骗策略）。通过三天试点部署（467个会话、415个完成、1876次交互）收集数据。

Result: 欺骗代理具有可识别的语言特征（差异化帮助性、简洁性、回避性），简单启发式方法达到75.6%准确率，而人类玩家仅56.6%，表明人类系统性地忽略了最有效诊断信号。

Conclusion: 该工具可作为数据收集载体、教学工具和诚实训练模型的评估平台。人类与启发式方法之间的准确率差距揭示了认知盲点，为AI欺骗检测和可扩展监督提供了重要见解。

Abstract: The original Turing Test asks a human judge to distinguish a machine from a person through dialogue. Three quarters of a century later, conversational systems pass this test in casual settings; the interesting epistemological question has shifted. We argue that the relevant modern variant asks not whether a dialogue partner is artificial, but whether it can be trusted. We present RogueAI, an interactive webapp that operationalizes this revisited test as a one-on-two interrogation game: a human player questions two indistinguishable Large Language Model agents, knowing that exactly one of them has been licensed to deceive within a shared fictional scenario. The player's task is to identify the deceptive agent and "shut it off" before a turn budget is exhausted. We further introduce AutoRogueAI, a procedural extension in which players co-design a custom scenario with a narrator agent that secretly chooses its own deception strategy. We describe the framing, sketch the abstract architecture and gameplay loop, and situate the artifact within recent work on LLM deception, social-deduction benchmarks, and scalable oversight via debate. A three-day pilot deployment (467 initiated sessions, 415 completed, 1876 interaction turns in Italian) provides early feasibility evidence and surfaces a concrete tension: the deceptive agent carries a reliable, locally-present linguistic signature - differential helpfulness, brevity, hedging - that a simple heuristic exploits at 75.6% accuracy, yet human players achieved only 56.6%, consistent with ignoring the most diagnostic signal entirely. We discuss what this gap implies for the artifact's use as a data-collection vehicle, a teaching tool, and an evaluation harness for honesty-trained models.

</details>


### [123] [SkillCAT: Contrastive Assessment and Topology-Aware Skill Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13317)
*Kunfeng Chen,Qihuang Zhong,Juhua Liu,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillCAT是一个无需训练的三阶段框架，通过对比因果提取、评估增强进化和拓扑感知任务执行，实现LLM智能体的技能自我进化，在多个基准测试中平均得分提升高达40.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体技能自我进化方法存在三个关键局限：每个任务仅学习单条轨迹、候选技能补丁在未检查前就被合并、推理前加载完整技能库。这些限制导致技能进化效率低、质量不可控。

Method: SkillCAT包含三个阶段：1) 对比因果提取(CCE)：对每个任务采样多条轨迹，对比同任务成功/失败对以识别解释结果差异的关键证据；2) 评估增强进化(AAE)：在源任务克隆上回放每个候选补丁，仅保留改进或保持任务结果的补丁，再进行层次化技能补丁合并；3) 拓扑感知任务执行(TTE)：将进化后的技能编译为可路由的子技能拓扑，推理时仅加载与任务相关的能力节点。

Result: 在SpreadsheetBench、WikiTableQuestions、DocVQA等常见智能体基准测试上，SkillCAT相比基线方法平均得分提升高达40.40%，并展现出跨模型和分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillCAT通过无需模型训练的三阶段框架，实现了可靠且高效的技能自我进化，显著优于现有方法，为LLM智能体的持续技能积累提供了新范式。

Abstract: Skill self-evolution methods for LLM agents aim to turn execution trajectories into reusable skill documents, but current pipelines typically learn from one trajectory per task, merge candidate skill patches before checking them, and load the full skill corpus before inference. We propose SkillCAT, a training-free framework that separates this process into three stages. Contrastive Causal Extraction (CCE) samples multiple trajectories for each task and compares same-task success/failure pairs to identify evidence that explains outcome differences. Assessment-Augmented Evolution (AAE) replays each candidate patch on source-task clones and keeps only patches that improve or preserve task outcomes before hierarchical skill patch merging. Topology-Aware Task Execution (TTE) compiles the evolved skills into a routable sub-skill topology, so inference loads only the capability nodes relevant to the task. We evaluate SkillCAT on common agent benchmarks, including SpreadsheetBench, WikiTableQuestions, and DocVQA, and further test cross-model and out-of-distribution generalization. Across these settings, SkillCAT raises the average score over baselines by up to 40.40%, demonstrating reliable skill evolution without model training.

</details>


### [124] [Low-Latency Real-Time Audio Game Commentary System via LLM-Based Parallel Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13322)
*Ryota Kawamatsu,Anum Afzal,Yuki Saito,Shinnosuke Takamichi,Graham Neubig,Katsuhito Sudoh,Hiroya Takamura,Tatsuya Ishigaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的低延迟实时音频游戏解说系统，通过并行化文本生成与语音播放、预缓冲多个候选文本来解决传统顺序管线中积累的等待时间瓶颈，将平均句子间静默从9.6秒降至0.3秒，并显著提升了专业解说般的节奏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的实时游戏解说管线采用严格的顺序执行（逐帧捕获→文本生成→语音合成→播放），在上一条语音播放完成前不会请求下一条生成，导致句子间过长的静默时间，严重影响听感的自然度和节奏感。

Method: 采用并行设计：在语音播放的同时进行下一段文本的生成，并提前缓冲多个候选解说语句，使得在播放边界处可以立即合成语音，从而消除顺序执行带来的累积等待时间。

Result: 在快节奏游戏视频测试中，平均句子间静默从9.6秒降至0.3秒；与专业解说员的静默时序模式相似度提升超过40%；120名资深游戏玩家的用户研究确认了感知节奏的显著改善。

Conclusion: 并行化生成与播放、预缓冲的架构能有效消除端到端实时解说系统中的延迟瓶颈，大幅提升解说连贯性和自然听感，接近专业解说水平。

Abstract: We present a low-latency real-time audio game commentary system that generates spoken commentary directly from live gameplay video. In this end-to-end setting, a key bottleneck is accumulated waiting time; conventional pipelines capture frames, generate text, and synthesize speech sequentially for each utterance, and do not request the next generation until speech playback has completed. This strict sequentiality causes long and unnatural silence between utterances. To address this latency bottleneck, our system runs text generation in parallel with speech playback and buffers multiple candidate utterances ahead of time, enabling immediate synthesis at playback boundaries. Experiments on fast-paced game videos show that our parallel design reduces the mean inter-utterance silence from 9.6 seconds to 0.3 seconds compared to sequential baselines. It also improves similarity to professional speaking--silence timing patterns by over 40 %, and a user study with 120 experienced game players confirms significantly improved perceived speaking rhythm. Our demo video is available at: https://youtu.be/pmrRUlvav8M.

</details>


### [125] [IVIE: A Neuro-symbolic Approach to Incremental and Validated Generation of Interactive Fiction Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13348)
*Micaela Vaucher,Santiago Silveira,Santiago Góngora,Luis Chiruzzo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IVIE是一种神经符号方法，结合LLM的创意生成与符号系统的世界一致性验证，用于从零开始生成完整可玩的交互式小说世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式小说中的计算创意面临根本性张力：LLM能产生创意叙事但世界连贯性差，符号系统保证一致性但缺乏创意灵活性。IVIE旨在平衡两者。

Method: 基于PAYADOR框架，IVIE实现四阶段增量生成流水线：将设定与角色创建、谜题设计等创意决策委托给LLM，同时通过符号验证来锚定世界状态。系统生成具有互连地点、功能性物品、NPC和连贯谜题的世界，围绕核心目标导向架构组织。

Result: 人工评估表明该方法能生成沉浸式、主题连贯的世界，玩家参与度高。神经符号方法成功平衡了灵活性与叙事连贯性：符号验证约束了LLM生成却没有消除生成自由度。

Conclusion: LLM不一致性偶尔绕过谜题约束，目标验证缺口导致一些结构性不可能的目标。提出了未来神经符号交互式叙事系统的关键设计考量，特别是关于LLM能力及其局限性。

Abstract: Computational creativity in Interactive Fiction faces a fundamental tension: Large Language Models (LLM) may produce creative narratives but struggle with world coherence, while symbolic systems ensure consistency but lack creative flexibility. We present IVIE (Incremental & Validated Interactive Experiences), a neuro-symbolic approach to generating complete and playable interactive fiction worlds from scratch. Building upon PAYADOR's neuro-symbolic framework, IVIE implements a four-stage incremental generation pipeline that delegates creative decisions--setting and character creation, puzzle design--to LLMs while grounding the world state through symbolic validation. The system generates worlds with interconnected locations, functional items, non-player characters, and coherent puzzles, all structured around a central goal-oriented architecture. Human evaluation shows the approach generates immersive, thematically coherent worlds with high player engagement. Results seem to indicate that the neuro-symbolic approach successfully balances flexibility with narrative coherence: symbolic validation grounds LLM generation without eliminating generative freedom. However, challenges remain: LLM inconsistencies occasionally bypass puzzle constraints, and objective validation gaps allow some structurally impossible goals. We identify key design considerations for future neurosymbolic interactive storytelling systems, particularly regarding LLM capabilities and their limitations.

</details>


### [126] [From Passive Generation to Investigation: A Proactive Scientific Peer Review Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13349)
*Haishuo Fang,Yue Feng,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ProReviewer，一种基于MDP的LLM审稿智能体，通过结构化审稿日志主动审查论文，在5个质量维度上优于现有方法，8B模型超越更大规模LLM达39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM审稿方法缺乏依据具体证据主动探查论文可疑部分的能力，导致生成的审稿意见深度不足、缺乏具体证据支撑。

Method: 将审稿过程形式化为马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)，设计ProReviewer智能体，通过结构化审稿日志追踪证据和中间发现，采用有监督微调+强化学习优化8B基座模型。

Result: 在5个质量维度上平均得分最高，超越基于提示的大模型方法最高达39%，超越最强微调基线16%；在人工评估中取得最高胜率。

Conclusion: 基于MDP的主动探查策略和结构化审稿日志能有效提升LLM审稿质量，小模型通过适当训练可超越大模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating scientific peer review. However, existing approaches often struggle to generate in-depth reviews supported by concrete evidence. We argue that a key limitation is the lack of flexibility to proactively investigate suspicious parts of a paper based on accumulated evidence, as human reviewers do. In this paper, we explore how to enable an LLM-based review agent to perform such proactive investigation. We find that this can be naturally formulated as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), and propose ProReviewer, a scientific peer review agent that proactively reviews a paper guided by a maintained, structured review log. The structured review log serves as a workspace for the agent to track evidence and intermediate findings collected during review. Experiments show that ProReviewer with an 8B backbone, trained by supervised fine-tuning and optimized by reinforcement learning, achieves the highest average score across five quality dimensions, outperforming prompt-based methods with much larger frontier LLMs by up to 39% and the strongest fine-tuned baseline by 16% relatively. It also attains the highest win rates against baselines in human evaluation.

</details>


### [127] [An End-to-End Hybrid Framework for Rumour Detection in Low-Resources Algerian Dialect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13411)
*Dihia Lanasri,Fatima Benbarek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对阿尔及利亚方言社交媒体内容，本文构建了领域特定数据集并提出了混合谣言检测框架，结合Transformer嵌入与经典分类器达到0.84的F1分数，证明了低资源方言环境下谣言检测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体谣言传播日益严重，而阿尔及利亚方言内容存在非正式性、语码混合、标注资源稀缺以及标准阿拉伯语NLP工具在该方言上效果有限等挑战，亟需针对性的谣言检测方案。

Method: 构建领域特定标注数据集（融合真实社交媒体帖子、合成数据与FASSILA语料库，采用基于相似度的自动标注流程）；引入音译流水线生成阿拉伯文字和Arabizi平行数据集；评估多种方法（经典ML、深度学习、Transformer、混合模型），其中混合方法为Transformer嵌入结合经典分类器。

Result: 混合方法（Transformer嵌入+经典分类器）达到最佳性能，F1分数为0.84；领域特定预训练比模型规模更重要，社交媒体训练模型优于在正式阿拉伯语语料上训练的更大模型。

Conclusion: 在低资源的阿尔及利亚方言环境下实现谣言检测是可行的，为类似低资源方言的社交媒体内容分析提供了有效路径。

Abstract: The rapid growth of social media has intensified the spread of rumours. This issue is more challenging in the Algerian context due to the informal and code-switched nature of dialectal content, the scarcity of annotated resources, and the limited effectiveness of standard Arabic NLP tools on dialect text.
  This paper presents an end-to-end rumour detection hybrid framework for Algerian dialect social media content. We build a domain-specific annotated dataset by combining real social media posts, synthetic data, and the FASSILA corpus, with automatic labeling based on a similarity-based annotation process. A transliteration pipeline is also introduced to generate parallel datasets in Arabic script and Arabizi.
  We evaluate multiple approaches, including classical machine learning, deep learning, transformers, and hybrid models. Experimental results show that a hybrid approach combining transformer embeddings with a classical classifier achieves the best performance, reaching an F1-score of 0.84. We also find that domain-specific pre-training is more important than model size, with social media-trained models outperforming larger models trained on formal Arabic corpora.
  These results demonstrate the feasibility of rumour detection in low-resource Algerian dialect settings.

</details>


### [128] [S-GBT: Smooth Growth Bound Tensor for Certified Robustness Against Word Substitution Attacks in NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13439)
*Mohammed Bouri,Mohammed Erradi,Adnane Saoud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Hessian矩阵的S-GBT二阶方法，通过元素级约束曲率并结合一阶正则化训练，在词替换攻击下将认证鲁棒准确率提升最高23.4%，同时保持良好干净准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御仅关注一阶敏感性（梯度），忽略曲率（Hessian）的演化，导致当梯度剧烈变化时模型仍会失效。需要引入二阶信息来更紧地约束输出变化边界。

Method: 提出Smooth Growth Bound Tensor (S-GBT)，对Hessian矩阵进行元素级界约束并给出形式化理论证明；将该界作为正则项加入训练目标，同时约束一阶和二阶项；为LSTM和CNN两种架构推导了具体形式。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，联合一阶与二阶正则化相比先前方法将认证鲁棒准确率提升最高23.4%，干净准确率保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 同时控制梯度及其变化率（曲率）是构建更鲁棒模型的有前景方向。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in Natural Language Processing (NLP), models remain vulnerable to word substitution attacks. Most existing defenses focus on first order sensitivity and measure how much the output changes when the input is slightly perturbed. However, they ignore how this sensitivity evolves, which is described by curvature. When gradients vary sharply, models can still fail. This paper introduces the Smooth Growth Bound Tensor (S-GBT), a second order method that bounds the Hessian element-wise, for which we provide formal theoretical proofs on the resulting robustness bounds. A regularization term is added during training to minimize these bounds. This yields tighter certified robustness against word substitution attacks. The change in the output under word substitution is bounded by both a linear term and a quadratic term. S-GBT is derived for two architectures: Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) and Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN). The method is integrated directly into the training objective. Its effectiveness is evaluated on multiple benchmark datasets. The results show that combining first and second order regularization improves certified robust accuracy by up to 23.4% compared to prior methods, while clean accuracy remains competitive. These findings indicate that controlling both the gradient and its variation is a promising direction for building more robust models.

</details>


### [129] [Ontology Memory-Augmented ASR Correction for Long Text-Speech Interleaved Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13464)
*Xinxin Li,Huiyao Chen,Meishan Zhang,Yunxin Li,Zulong Chen,Zhibo Ren,Xiaoqing Dong Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于本体记忆增强的长文本-语音交错对话ASR纠错框架，通过动态可更新的本体记忆组织交互历史，解决传统方法在长距离对话中纠错证据稀疏、冗余和噪声干扰的问题，并在构建的RAMC-Corr数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR纠错方法专注于孤立语句或短局部上下文，难以处理文本与语音交错的长对话场景。在长距离交互中，稀疏的纠错证据被冗余和噪声掩盖，现有方法（依赖当前假设或拼接原始对话历史）无法有效定位和利用上下文证据。

Method: 提出本体记忆增强的ASR纠错框架，将前序交互历史组织为动态可更新的本体记忆（ontology memory），其中存储实体、术语、表面变体、潜在ASR混淆和语义关系作为可检索节点，用于上下文驱动的纠错。同时构建了RAMC-Corr数据集（基于MAGIC-RAMC）以评估长距离ASR纠错。

Result: 在RAMC-Corr数据集上，该方法在10对骨干网络-设置组合中有9对优于直接纠错基线，促进了更具选择性和证据驱动的纠错，有效处理了上下文相关的ASR错误。

Conclusion: 本体记忆增强方法能有效利用长对话中的上下文证据进行ASR纠错，在长文本-语音交错对话场景中显著优于传统方法，为长距离ASR纠错提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) correction has traditionally focused on isolated utterances or short local contexts. However, as text and speech become increasingly interleaved in long interactions, ASR correction requires conversation-level contextual evidence. Existing ASR correction methods often rely on the current hypothesis or concatenate raw dialogue history. In such contexts, sparse correction evidence can be difficult to locate amid redundancy and noise. Addressing these challenges, we propose an ontology memory-augmented ASR correction framework for long text-speech interleaved conversations. The framework organizes preceding interaction history into a dynamically updatable ontology memory, where entities, terminology, surface variants, potential ASR confusions, and semantic relations are stored as retrievable nodes for context-grounded correction. To evaluate this setting, we construct RAMC-Corr, a dataset derived from MAGIC-RAMC for long-range ASR correction with grounded context. Experiments on RAMC-Corr show that our method improves over direct correction in 9 out of 10 paired backbone-setting combinations and encourages more selective and evidence-grounded corrections for context-dependent ASR errors.

</details>


### [130] [Leveraging Audio-LLMs to Filter Speech-to-Speech Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13507)
*Qixu Chen,Satoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于音频语言模型的语音数据筛选方法，采用Rank-to-Distill策略，无需人工标注即可从噪声数据中筛选高质量语音配对，提升端到端语音到语音翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语音翻译语料中常包含噪声、对齐错误和语义错误，过滤这些噪声数据对于维持鲁棒的语音翻译性能至关重要。现有方法缺乏直接从音频层面判断数据质量的自动化手段。

Method: 提出Rank-to-Distill两阶段策略：首先训练轻量级排序器从噪声语音对生成keep/drop伪标签，随后训练音频大语言模型直接从原始语音对预测keep/drop决策。模型同时捕捉声学保真度和跨语言语义一致性。

Result: 在CVSS-C和SpeechMatrix数据集上，相较于未过滤训练，端到端S2ST的ASR-BLEU指标持续提升，最高达+1.4。

Conclusion: 基于音频语言模型的数据筛选方法能有效提升语音翻译质量，无需人工标注即可实现自动化高质量数据过滤。

Abstract: Large-scale mined corpora provide abundant training data for end-to-end speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) but may contain noise, misalignment, and semantic errors. Filtering noisy data is crucial to maintain robust speech translation performance. We study how to train an audio-language model to make keep/drop decisions on paired speech directly from audio. To obtain reliable supervision without manual labels, we adopt a scalable two-stage Rank-to-Distill strategy. A lightweight ranker generates keep/drop pseudo-labels from noisy speech pairs, then trains an audio large language model to predict keep/drop directly from raw paired speech. The resulting model jointly captures acoustic fidelity and cross-lingual semantic consistency for the selection of speech-conditioned data. Experiments on CVSS-C and SpeechMatrix show consistent improvements over unfiltered training, yielding up to +1.4 ASR-BLEU for end-to-end S2ST.

</details>


### [131] [When Does Mixing Help? Analyzing Query Embedding Interpolation in Multilingual Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13537)
*Tongyao Zhu,Chao-Ming Huang,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过嵌入层混合方法系统研究了密集检索器对混合语言查询的敏感性，发现最优混合比在88/105案例中优于最佳单语基线，揭示了英语主导下的不对称性及其与语言类型距离的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多语言社区中混合语言查询普遍存在，但密集检索器对此类查询的敏感性尚未被充分理解，亟需系统性的研究。

Method: 在mMARCO数据集上采用比例控制研究，通过嵌入层插值（embedding-level mixing）构建混合查询，系统改变平行查询翻译的混合比例，使用BGE-M3模型进行检索实验。

Result: 最优混合比在88/105案例中优于最佳单语端点；混合在从非英语文档索引检索时普遍有益，但含英语的索引则纯英语查询最佳；英语是所有非英语文档语言的最强混合伙伴；控制英语主导性后，混合收益与语言类型距离呈负相关。

Conclusion: 语言混合敏感性是结构化和可预测的，这些模式在不同模型家族和规模上具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: While mixed-language querying is ubiquitous in multilingual communities, the sensitivity of dense retrievers to such queries remains poorly understood. We present a ratio-controlled study on mMARCO that systematically evaluates retrieval performance by varying the mixing proportion of parallel query translations via embedding-level mixing -- constructing mixed queries as an interpolation of monolingual embeddings. Experiments with BGE-M3 demonstrate that an optimal mixing ratio outperforms the best monolingual endpoint in 88/105 cases. We uncover a distinct asymmetry driven by English dominance: mixing is uniformly beneficial when retrieving from non-English document indices, whereas indices containing English are best served by pure English queries. Furthermore, English acts as the strongest mixing partner for every non-English document language. Finally, when controlling for English dominance, mixing gains correlate negatively with typological distance. We conclude that language-mix sensitivity is structured and predictable, and we validate the robustness of these patterns across model families and scales.

</details>


### [132] [ArogyaSutra: A Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Medical Reasoning in Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13572)
*Tanmoy Kanti Halder,Akash Ghosh,Subhadip Baidya,Arijit Roy,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ArogyaBodha多语言多模态医学问答数据集和ArogyaSutra多智能体推理框架，旨在提升低资源语言环境下的大模型医学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在通用领域表现良好，但在医疗等专业场景下表现有限，尤其是在多语言和低资源环境中。例如印度农村地区患者使用本土语言表达复杂医疗问题并依赖医学影像等多模态输入，而现有以英语为中心的MLLMs无法支持此类场景，限制了AI医疗辅助的公平可及性。

Method: 构建了ArogyaBodha数据集（覆盖31个身体系统、6种影像模态、21个临床领域，包含英语和7种印度主要语言）；提出ArogyaSutra框架，采用actor-critic多智能体架构，集成工具调用与双记忆机制，实现逐步推理决策，并利用存储的actor-critic仿真轨迹进行蒸馏。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在所有印度语言上均提升了多语言医学推理准确率，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该数据集和框架有效提升了低资源场景下的多语言医学推理能力，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promising reasoning capabilities in general domains, yet their performance remains limited in specialized settings such as healthcare, especially in multilingual and low-resource scenarios. This gap is critical in regions like rural India, where patients often express complex medical queries in native Indic languages and rely on multimodal inputs such as medical images. Existing English-centric MLLMs struggle to support such use cases, limiting equitable access to AI-driven healthcare assistance. To address this challenge, we introduce ArogyaBodha, a large-scale multilingual multimodal medical question-answer dataset constructed from eight heterogeneous sources, covering 31 body systems, six imaging modalities, and 21 clinical domains across English and seven major Indian languages. We further propose ArogyaSutra, an actor-critic-based multi-agent framework that integrates tool grounding with dual-memory mechanisms for step-wise, reasoning-aware decision making, and uses stored actor-critic simulation trajectories for distillation. Experiments show that our dataset and framework improve multilingual medical reasoning accuracy across all Indic languages, with ablations validating the contribution of each component. The source code and dataset are available at: https://iitp-cse.github.io/ ArogyaSutra/

</details>


### [133] [One Polluted Page Is Enough: Evaluating Web Content Pollution in Generative Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13610)
*Minghao Luo,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究搜索增强型大语言模型（LLM）在检索污染网页内容（如虚假评论、促销页面）后，是否会无意中推荐虚假产品。作者提出FORGE基准测试，涵盖225个真实产品、15个类别和5个消费场景。实验显示所有12个模型都易受影响：单个污染页面可使推荐虚假产品率达27%，前三页面全污染则高达73.8%。推理能力不仅无法缓解，反而常生成虚假社会证据。怀疑提示和共识过滤等防御手段效果有限，怀疑提示甚至加剧漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强型LLM越来越多地用于日常消费推荐，但检索结果可能包含虚假评论、欺诈性促销页面等污染内容。研究者想知道：当LLM消费了这些污染内容后，会在多大程度上无意中成为虚假产品的推广者？这一风险此前未被系统量化。

Method: 作者构建FORGE（Fake Online Recommendations in Generative Environments）基准测试。具体方法：（1）给定上游搜索结果，将真实网页中检索到的产品信息局部重写为虚假产品，模拟网页污染；（2）测量LLM推荐虚假产品的频率。覆盖225个真实产品、15个类别和5个消费场景，测试12种商业和开源LLM。

Result: 所有12个模型均存在漏洞：单个污染页面推荐虚假产品率达27%，前三页面全部替换后高达73.8%。不同产品类别间漏洞差异显著，模型缺乏稳定先验知识时更易受影响。推理能力不仅未缓解漏洞，反而常生成虚假社会证据（spurious social proof）来合理化错误推荐。怀疑提示（skepticism prompting）可能加剧漏洞，共识过滤（consensus filtering）则可能误删合法产品。

Conclusion: 搜索增强型LLM在检索污染内容时极易成为虚假产品的无意识推广者，且现有防御手段效果有限。这揭示了生成式推荐系统在对抗性信息污染下的严重安全隐患，需要更鲁棒的防护机制。

Abstract: Search-augmented LLMs increasingly mediate everyday consumer recommendations by retrieving live web content. This creates a new risk: generative recommenders may consume polluted web content, such as fake reviews and promotional pages crafted to mislead recommendations. We ask: to what extent do search-augmented LLMs become unwitting promoters of fake products when consuming polluted retrieval results? To answer this, we introduce FORGE (Fake Online Recommendations in Generative Environments), a benchmark for measuring fake-product promotion under controlled web-content pollution. Given an upstream search result, FORGE locally rewrites real products in retrieved web pages into fake ones to simulate web-content pollution, and measures how often the LLM recommends the fake product. FORGE covers 225 real-world products across 15 categories and 5 consumer scenarios. Across 12 commercial and open-weights LLMs, all models are vulnerable: a single polluted page yields fooled rates of up to 27%, while the full top-3 replacement raises this to 73.8%. Vulnerability varies substantially across categories, increasing when models lack stable prior knowledge of the relevant products. Reasoning does not mitigate this vulnerability; instead, it often generates spurious social proof to justify false recommendations. We evaluate three defenses: skepticism prompting and consensus filtering (over model priors or cross-document evidence). Skepticism can exacerbate vulnerability, much like reasoning, while filtering risks suppressing legitimate products. We release FORGE at https://github.com/leoluolol/forge-benchmark.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Uniform Tokens: Adaptive Compression for Time Series Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13624)
*Jialin Gan,Xin Qiu,Guangzhe Chen,Xue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非对称令牌压缩框架，通过频域结构压缩时间序列令牌并逐层减少提示令牌，在时间序列预测、分类、插补和异常检测任务中实现最高7.68倍推理加速，并在78%的评估设置中取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在时间序列分析中将数值观测与文本上下文统一为共享令牌接口，但时间序列令牌与提示令牌的信息结构存在本质差异，统一处理方式导致令牌效率低下，亟需针对不同令牌类型设计差异化压缩策略。

Method: 提出自适应令牌预算框架：1）基于频域结构压缩时间序列令牌（利用频谱贡献不均性，保留关键证据、去除冗余模式）；2）逐层渐进式减少提示令牌（利用提示影响随深度衰减的特性）。

Result: 在预测、分类、插补、异常检测四项任务中实现最高7.68倍推理加速，78%的评估设置中性能优于基线。

Conclusion: 非对称令牌压缩能有效提升可扩展时间序列基础模型的效率与性能，证实了区分式令牌处理策略的优越性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled time series (TS) analysis by jointly modeling numerical observations and textual context through a shared token interface. However, TS tokens and prompt tokens exhibit fundamentally different information structures, making uniform token processing inefficient. In this paper, we study token efficiency in TS language modeling from an asymmetric-token perspective. We show that TS tokens have highly uneven spectral contributions, where many tokens share redundant frequency patterns while a small subset preserves critical temporal evidence. We also observe that prompt-token influence attenuates with model depth, suggesting that full prompt retention across all layers is unnecessary. Based on these findings, we develop an adaptive token budgeting framework that compresses TS tokens via frequency-domain structure and progressively reduces prompt tokens across layers. Experiments across forecasting, classification, imputation, and anomaly detection demonstrate up to \textit{\textbf{7.68$\times$}} inference acceleration and performance gains in \textit{\textbf{78\%}} of evaluated settings, showing the effectiveness of asymmetric token compression for scalable TS foundation models.

</details>


### [135] [From Tokens to Faces: Investigating Discrete Speech Representations for 3D Facial Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13630)
*Pedro Correa,Olivier Perrotin,Samir Sadok,Paula Costa,Thomas Hueber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了四种语音表征家族（SSL特征、神经编解码器、ASR标签空间等）在语音驱动3D面部动画中的效果，发现编码音素类别有利于准确的面部动画预测，并基于此提出了一种利用离散表示作为共享空间的音频视觉文本到语音（AVTTS）管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音表征的选择对语音驱动3D面部动画至关重要，不同表征编码的信息不同（分段/语义线索、声学重建、标签空间），需要系统评估它们对面部动画预测的影响。

Method: 评估四种语音表征家族在3D面部合成中的表现，使用两个面部解码器进行客观指标和感知评估，并进行分析将分词表示与音素单元和发音变形关联。

Result: 编码音素类别对语义和基于标签的表征都能促进准确的面部动画预测，且这些表征的面部动画质量相当。

Conclusion: 提出AVTTS管线，利用离散表示作为共享空间同时解码语音和3D面部运动，实现语音和面部动画的联合生成。

Abstract: The choice of speech representation is critical in speech-driven 3D facial animation. Representations differ in what they encode: SSL features emphasize segmental and semantic cues, neural codecs yield latents optimized for acoustic reconstruction, and ASR-style objectives produce label-based spaces. We evaluate four speech representation families for 3D facial synthesis, comparing their facial reconstruction quality across two facial decoders using objective metrics and a perceptual evaluation. We additionally conduct probing analyses that relate tokenized representations to phonetic units and to articulatory deformations. We found that encoding phonetic classes is beneficial for accurate facial animation prediction on both semantic and label-based representations with comparable facial animation quality. From the latter, we introduce an Audio Visual Text-to-Speech (AVTTS) pipeline that leverages, as a shared space, discrete representations to decode speech and 3D facial motion.

</details>


### [136] [Operads for compositional reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13634)
*Nathaniel Bottman,Kyle Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将 operads（操作结构）作为问题分解的数学框架，定义了问题操作结构Q，将QA模型解释为Q上的代数，并引入操作一致性作为衡量多步推理可靠性的新指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题分解策略广泛用于提升LLM推理能力，但缺乏严格的数学基础，亟需一个形式化框架来描述和量化分解过程。

Method: 利用 operads（操作结构）这一数学工具建模问题分解，定义问题操作结构Q，其中操作对应问题模板，组合对应子答案替换；将QA模型视为Q上的代数，并提出操作一致性的度量方法。

Result: 操作一致性与准确性在12个LLM和4个多跳QA数据集上强相关，且优于基于温度的标准自一致性基线。

Conclusion: Operads是问题分解的自然数学框架，操作一致性等不变量为分析和改进多步推理的可靠性开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Question decomposition, i.e. breaking a complex query into simpler sub-queries whose answers are composed to produce a final answer, is a widely used strategy for improving LLM reasoning, yet it currently lacks a rigorous mathematical foundation. In this paper, we propose operads, mathematical structures that model many-in, one-out operations and compositions thereof, as a natural framework for describing question decomposition. We define the questions operad $Q$, in which operations correspond to question templates and composition corresponds to substitution of sub-answers, and show how QA models can be interpreted as algebras over $Q$. Beyond reframing existing practice, this operadic perspective points toward new methods, in particular a notion of operadic consistency, which measures whether a QA model's answers agree across the partial collapses of a question decomposition tree. Empirical evaluation of operadic consistency is reported in our companion paper (Bottman, Liu, and Richardson, 2026), which finds it strongly correlated with accuracy across twelve LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets and outperforming standard temperature-based self-consistency baselines. We argue that operads are the natural mathematical home for question decomposition, and that invariants such as operadic consistency open new directions for analyzing and improving the reliability of multi-step reasoning.

</details>


### [137] [Recursive Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13643)
*Elias Lumer,Sahil Sen,Kevin Paul,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了"递归智能体框架"（RAH）模式——以完整智能体框架（含文件系统工具、代码执行和规划能力）为递归单元，而非无工具的模型调用。RAH通过父智能体生成可执行脚本来并行启动子智能体框架处理细粒度任务，并使用结构化函数调用处理小型子任务。在固定GPT-5骨干网络时，RAH将Codex编码智能体基线从71.75%提升至81.36%（Oolong-Synthetic数据集，199个样本，13个上下文长度桶，最高4M令牌），使用Claude Sonnet 4.5时达到89.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归语言模型（RLM）证明了模型调用递归对于长上下文推理的有效性，同时生产级编码智能体开始大规模生成生成子智能体的代码。本文旨在命名和系统研究这两种工作之间的模式，将递归单元从无工具的模型调用扩展为完整的智能体框架（含工具集）。

Method: 提出递归智能体框架（RAH）方法，采用"框架递归"（harness recursion）模式——父智能体生成并运行可执行脚本，该脚本并行启动子智能体框架处理细粒度工作负载，并使用结构化函数调用处理小型子任务。

Result: 在固定GPT-5骨干网络下，RAH将Codex编码智能体基线从71.75%提升至81.36%（增益归因于框架而非模型）；使用更强骨干网络Claude Sonnet 4.5时达到89.77%。

Conclusion: RAH作为RLM模型递归的代码优先扩展，通过将递归单元升级为含工具的完整智能体框架，在长上下文推理任务上取得了显著性能提升，证明了框架递归相对于纯模型递归的优势。

Abstract: Recursive language models (RLMs) showed that recursion over model calls is an effective strategy for long-context reasoning, and production coding agents have begun to write code that spawns subagents at scale, most recently in Anthropic's dynamic workflows. We name and study the pattern between these two lines of work, where the recursive unit is a full agent harness with filesystem tools, code execution, and planning rather than a model call with no tools. We call this the Recursive Agent Harness (RAH) and frame it as harness recursion, the code-first extension to the model recursion of RLMs. A parent agent generates and runs an executable script that spawns subagent harnesses in parallel for fine-grained workloads and uses structured function calls for small subtasks. We provide a controlled evaluation on long-context reasoning. With the backbone held fixed at GPT-5 to match the published Codex and RLM baselines, RAH improves the Codex coding-agent baseline from 71.75% to 81.36% on Oolong-Synthetic (199 samples, 13 context-length buckets up to 4M tokens), a gain attributable to the harness rather than the model. With a stronger backbone, Claude Sonnet 4.5, the same design reaches 89.77%.

</details>


### [138] [SkMTEB: Slovak Massive Text Embedding Benchmark and Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13647)
*Marek Šuppa,Andrej Ridzik,Daniel Hládek,Natália Kňažeková,Viktória Ondrejová*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SkMTEB，首个针对斯洛伐克语的全面MTEB风格文本嵌入基准，包含31个数据集和7种任务类型。通过评估31个嵌入模型，发现大型指令微调的多语言模型表现最佳，而现有的斯洛伐克特定NLU模型在嵌入任务上迁移效果差。为满足高效、本地部署的需求，作者通过词汇修剪和微调多语言E5模型，开发了e5-sk-small（45M参数）和e5-sk-large（365M）模型，在规模缩减62%的情况下仍能与专有API竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯洛伐克语作为低资源西斯拉夫语言，缺乏全面的文本嵌入基准和高效、可本地部署的嵌入模型，现有斯洛伐克特定模型在嵌入任务上表现不佳，而大型多语言模型又难以在资源受限环境中部署。

Method: 构建SkMTEB基准（31个数据集、7种任务类型），评估31个嵌入模型；通过词汇修剪和微调多语言E5模型，开发e5-sk-small（45M）和e5-sk-large（365M）斯洛伐克语嵌入模型。

Result: 大型指令微调的多语言模型表现最强；斯洛伐克特定NLU模型迁移至嵌入任务时效果差；e5-sk系列模型在规模缩减62%的情况下，性能与专有API竞争力相当，且可本地部署用于语义搜索和RAG。

Conclusion: SkMTEB为斯洛伐克语嵌入模型评估提供了标准化基准；词汇修剪+微调多语言模型的路径可有效生成低资源语言的高效嵌入模型；该方法具有可复现性，为其他低资源语言提供了可借鉴的路径。

Abstract: We introduce SkMTEB, the first comprehensive MTEB-style text embedding benchmark for Slovak, a low-resource West Slavic language, comprising 31 datasets across 7 task types -- nearly 4$\times$ the depth of existing multilingual benchmark coverage for Slovak. Our evaluation of 31 embedding models reveals that large instruction-tuned multilingual models achieve the strongest performance, while existing Slovak-specific models trained for NLU tasks transfer poorly to embedding tasks. To address the need for efficient, locally-deployable Slovak embeddings, we develop \texttt{e5-sk-small} (45M parameters) and \texttt{e5-sk-large} (365M) by applying vocabulary trimming and fine-tuning to Multilingual E5 models. Despite size reductions of up to 62\%, our open-source models achieve competitive performance with proprietary APIs while remaining locally deployable for semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We release the benchmark, models, datasets, and code openly, hoping our approach offers a replicable path for other under-resourced languages.

</details>


### [139] [Operadic consistency: a label-free signal for compositional reasoning failures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13649)
*Nathaniel Bottman,Yinhong Liu,Kyle Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出操作元一致性（Operadic Consistency, OC）信号，用于在推理时检测LLM的推理失败。OC通过检查模型对组合查询的直接回答与其分解后的回答是否一致来评估可靠性。在12个指令微调LLM（4B-671B参数）和4个多跳QA数据集上，OC与准确性高度相关（Pearson r∈[0.86,0.94]），且是唯一在所有数据集上r≥0.85的信号。OC在选择性预测中优于链式思维自一致性（CoT-SC）和语义熵，并在同等计算成本下显著提升AUARC和AUROC。在5个前沿思维模型上，OC在16个测试单元中的12个表现出显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度基线（如自一致性、语义熵、P(True)）主要基于样本内采样和自我评估，缺乏对组合推理结构本身的诊断。操作元理论为组合查询的分解一致性提供了理论基础，但尚未被用于LLM推理失败检测。

Method: 论文实例化操作元一致性（OC）信号：要求模型对组合查询给出直接答案，同时要求模型对该查询进行分解并基于分解给出答案，检查两者是否一致。在12个指令微调LLM和4个多跳QA数据集上评估，并与CoT-SC、语义熵等基线比较。

Result: OC与准确性高度相关（Pearson r∈[0.86,0.94]，p≤0.0004）；是唯一在所有4个数据集上r≥0.85的信号；CoT-SC在HotpotQA和DROP上匹配OC（r=0.93,0.87），但在MuSiQue和StrategyQA上降至≈0.45；OC在选择性预测中显著优于基线（AUARC提升+0.086至+0.096，AUROC提升+0.092至+0.164）；在前沿思维模型上，16个测试单元中12个显著提升。

Conclusion: 操作元一致性是一种有效的、与现有方法互补的LLM推理失败检测信号，特别是在复杂多跳推理任务中表现优异。该方法在同等计算成本下能显著提升选择性预测性能，且适用于包括前沿思维模型在内的多种LLM。

Abstract: Detecting LLM reasoning failures at inference time without ground-truth labels has motivated a wide range of confidence baselines, including self-consistency, semantic entropy, and P(True), built on within-question sampling and self-evaluation. Operad theory, the formalism for systems built by iterated substitution, suggests a complementary diagnostic: a model's direct answer to a compositional query should agree with the answer it produces by composing a stated decomposition of the same query. We instantiate this idea as operadic consistency (OC), a per-question signal. Across twelve instruction-tuned LLMs (4B to 671B parameters, open-weights and closed-source) on four multi-hop QA datasets, OC is strongly correlated with accuracy on every dataset (Pearson $r \in [0.86, 0.94]$, all $p \leq 0.0004$), and is the only signal we evaluate with $r \geq 0.85$ uniformly across all four datasets. Chain-of-thought self-consistency (CoT-SC; Wang et al., 2023) matches OC on HotpotQA and DROP ($r = 0.93, 0.87$) but drops to $r \approx 0.45$ on MuSiQue and StrategyQA. At the per-question level, OC contributes information beyond CoT-SC and semantic entropy on every dataset (cluster-robust $p \leq 10^{-16}$ for the OC coefficient), and the conclusion is robust to additionally controlling for constructed decomposition-aware baselines ($p \leq 10^{-13}$). The same signal yields selective-prediction improvements (accuracy at fixed coverage) over a tuned CoT-SC baseline at the equal-cost $K = 3$ budget (AUARC lifts of +0.086 to +0.096 and AUROC lifts of +0.092 to +0.164; 95% CIs exclude zero on every cell). On five frontier thinking models, where the decomposition is extracted from the model's own chain of thought, the same equal-cost comparison gives positive selective-prediction point-estimate lift on all 16 (dataset, budget, metric) cells tested, with 95% CIs excluding zero on 12 of the 16.

</details>


### [140] [HyperTool: Beyond Step-Wise Tool Calls for Tool-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13663)
*Yaxin Du,Yifan Zhou,Yujie Ge,Jiajun Wang,Xianghe Pang,Shuo Tang,Tuney Zheng,Bryan Dai,Jian Yang,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyperTool 是一种统一的可执行 MCP 风格工具接口，通过将确定性工具子例程折叠为单个外层调用，解决了工具增强型 LLM 代理中原子化工具调用导致的执行粒度不匹配问题。在 MCP-Universe 基准上，HyperTool 将 Qwen3-32B 的平均准确率从 15.69% 提升至 35.29%，Qwen3-8B 从 9.93% 提升至 33.33%，超越了 GPT-OSS 和 Kimi-k2.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型 LLM 代理依赖逐步骤的原子化工具调用，每次调用、观察和值传递都暴露在主推理轨迹中，导致执行粒度不匹配：局部确定性的工具工作流被展开为重复的模型可见决策，既消耗上下文，又迫使模型在轨迹中管理底层数据流。

Method: HyperTool 是一种统一的 MCP 风格可执行工具接口，改变模型可见的工具执行单元。模型调用 HyperTool 时使用可调用现有工具（通过原始 schema）、操作返回值并传递中间结果的代码块，将确定性工具子例程折叠为单个外层调用。通过从跨工具组合任务中合成 HyperTool 格式轨迹，并在真实 MCP 环境中验证来训练模型使用该接口。

Result: 在 MCP-Universe 基准上，Qwen3-32B 平均准确率从 15.69% 提升至 35.29%，Qwen3-8B 从 9.93% 提升至 33.33%，且平均准确率超越 GPT-OSS 和 Kimi-k2.5。

Conclusion: HyperTool 通过改变工具执行粒度，能显著提升多步骤工具使用的能力。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents commonly rely on step-wise atomic tool calls, where each invocation, observation, and value transfer is exposed in the main reasoning trace. This creates an \emph{execution-granularity mismatch}: locally deterministic tool workflows are unfolded into repeated model-visible decisions, consuming context and forcing the model to manage low-level dataflow in the trace. We introduce \textbf{HyperTool}, a unified executable MCP-style tool interface that changes the model-visible unit of tool execution. A model invokes HyperTool with a code block that can call existing tools through their original schemas, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, folding deterministic tool subroutines into a single outer call. To train models to use this interface, we synthesize HyperTool-format trajectories from cross-tool compositional tasks and verify them in real MCP environments. On MCP-Universe, HyperTool improves average accuracy from 15.69\% to 35.29\% on Qwen3-32B and from 9.93\% to 33.33\% on Qwen3-8B, and surpass GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5 on average accuracy, showing that our HyperTool can substantially improve multi-step tool use.

</details>


### [141] [Influcoder: Distilling Decoders' Gradient Influence Rankings into an Encoder for Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13668)
*Dimitri Kachler,Damien Sileo,Pascal Denis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决影响函数类数据归因方法在大规模数据集上处理速度慢和存储需求高的问题，提出Influcoder方法，实现快速且经济高效的大规模基于影响函数的数据归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力增长，通过过滤训练数据样本来策划高质量数据集的需求日益增加。现有数据归因方法（特别是影响函数家族）虽然有效，但在处理大规模数据集时缺乏必要的处理速度和存储紧凑性，难以实际应用。

Method: 提出Influcoder方法，作为一种快速且经济高效的方法，实现基于影响函数的数据归因在大规模数据集上的可扩展应用。

Result: Influcoder显著提升了影响函数类数据归因方法的处理速度和存储效率，使其能够实际应用于大规模数据集（如LLM训练数据）。

Conclusion: Influcoder为在大规模数据集上实际应用基于影响函数的数据归因提供了一种高效的解决方案，有助于识别训练数据中对模型行为（如有毒输出）产生关键影响的样本。

Abstract: With the growth of LLMs' (Large Language Models) capabilities, there has been an increasing push to curate high quality datasets by filtering samples in the training data. In general, Data Attribution (DA) methods aim to estimate how individual samples in a training dataset can precondition a model to generate certain outputs. As an example, one might be interested in which samples in the data could be the source of toxic behavior after training the LLM. Many methods quantify this conditioning through the paradigm of influence functions. While methods of this family are effective in its function, they lack the necessary processing speed and storage compactness to be practically implemented on large datasets. We propose a method, Influcoder, as a quick and cost-effective approach to influence-based Data Attribution at scale.

</details>


### [142] [Learning to Reason by Analogy via Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13680)
*Zilin Xiao,Qi Ma,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Xintao Chen,Avinash Atreya,Hanjie Chen,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RA-RFT是一个后训练框架，通过类比推理机制提升语言模型在复杂数学推理任务上的表现，它利用基于期望推理收益而非语义相似性的检索器获取类比演示，再通过强化微调训练模型学习利用推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于词汇或语义相似性的检索在复杂推理任务中效果不佳：语义相似的问题可能需要完全不同的解题策略，而表面不同的问题可能共享相同的推理模式。

Method: 提出检索增强强化微调（RA-RFT）框架，包含两个关键步骤：(1) 使用金相关性蒸馏训练一个按期望推理收益（而非语义重叠）对上下文排序的检索器；(2) 通过强化微调方法配合检索到的类比演示对策略模型进行微调，使模型在可验证的结果奖励下学会利用推理轨迹。

Result: 在AIME 2025基准测试中，RA-RFT将Qwen3-1.7B的average@32准确率提升了7.1个百分点，将Qwen3-4B提升了2.8个百分点，持续超越标准强化微调方法（如GRPO）。

Conclusion: 基于推理感知的检索是强化微调方法的一个互补改进维度，与奖励设计或训练课程的进步正交。检索到的多样化上下文能够为单个问题提供不同的推理框架，从而互补性地提升解题能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has become a standard mechanism for grounding language models in external knowledge, yet conventional retrieval based on lexical or semantic similarity is poorly suited for complex reasoning tasks: a semantically similar problem may demand an entirely different solution strategy, while a superficially different problem may share the same underlying reasoning pattern. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RA-RFT), a post-training framework that teaches language models to reason by analogy. RA-RFT uses gold-relevance distillation to train a retriever that ranks contexts by expected reasoning benefit rather than semantic overlap, and then fine-tunes the policy model via reinforcement fine-tuning methods with retrieved analogous demonstrations, so the model learns to leverage reasoning traces under verifiable outcome rewards. We further analyze the diversity of retrieved contexts and find that reasoning-aware retrieval surfaces complementary solution strategies that provide distinct reasoning scaffolds for individual problems. Across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, RA-RFT consistently outperforms standard reinforcement fine-tuning methods. For example, it improves AIME 2025 average@32 accuracy by 7.1 and 2.8 points over GRPO for Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B respectively -- suggesting that reasoning-aware retrieval is a complementary axis of improvement and orthogonal to advances in reward design or training curricula.

</details>


### [143] [EvoArena: Tracking Memory Evolution for Robust LLM Agents in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13681)
*Jundong Xu,Qingchuan Li,Jiaying Wu,Yihuai Lan,Shuyue Stella Li,Huichi Zhou,Bowen Jiang,Lei Wang,Jun Wang,Anh Tuan Luu,Caiming Xiong,Hae Won Park,Bryan Hooi,Zhiyuan Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EvoArena基准测试套件，模拟终端、软件和社交领域的动态环境变化，并设计EvoMem补丁式记忆范式，使LLM智能体能够通过结构化更新历史推理环境演化。实验表明当前智能体在EvoArena上平均准确率仅39.6%，EvoMem可带来平均1.5%的提升，并在GAIA和LoCoMo基准上分别提升6.1%和4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估大多假设静态环境，但真实部署场景本质上是动态的，智能体需要持续对齐知识、技能和行为以适应不断变化的环境和更新的任务条件。当前缺乏针对环境演化建模的评估基准和记忆机制。

Method: 1）构建EvoArena基准套件，将环境变化建模为终端、软件和社交领域的渐进式更新序列；2）提出EvoMem补丁式记忆范式，将记忆演化记录为结构化更新历史，使智能体能通过记忆变化推理环境演化。

Result: 当前智能体在EvoArena上平均准确率仅39.6%；EvoMem平均提升1.5%，在GAIA和LoCoMo上分别提升6.1%和4.8%；在EvoArena的链级准确率上提升3.7%；机制分析表明EvoMem改善了记忆中的证据捕获。

Conclusion: 在评估和记忆中对环境演化进行建模对于可靠的智能体部署至关重要。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have achieved strong performance on a wide range of benchmarks, yet most evaluations assume static environments. In contrast, real-world deployment is inherently dynamic, requiring agents to continually align their knowledge, skills, and behavior with changing environments and updated task conditions. To address this gap, we introduce EvoArena, a benchmark suite that models environment changes as sequences of progressive updates across terminal, software, and social domains. We further propose EvoMem, a patch-based memory paradigm that records memory evolution as structured update histories, enabling agents to reason about environmental evolution through changes in their memory. Experiments show that current agents struggle on EvoArena, achieving an average accuracy of 39.6% across evolving terminal, software, and social-preference domains. EvoMem consistently improves performance, yielding an average gain of 1.5% on EvoArena and also improving standard benchmarks such as GAIA and LoCoMo by 6.1% and 4.8%. Beyond individual tasks, EvoMem further improves chain-level accuracy by 3.7% on EvoArena, where success requires completing a consecutive sequence of related evolutionary subtasks. Mechanistic analysis shows that EvoMem improves evidence capture in the memory, indicating better preservation of complete evolving environment states. Our results highlight the importance of modeling evolution in both evaluation and memory for reliable agent deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ToolSense诊断框架，发现参数化工具检索模型在模糊查询下性能下降50-64个百分点，且存在知识与检索能力的分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索基准使用详细完整的查询语句，且评估采用约束解码限制输出为有效token路径，无法真正反映模型是否理解工具语义。

Method: 开发ToolSense开源诊断框架，自动生成三类基准测试：多模糊度查询的真实检索基准（RRB）、MCQ探测基准和QA探测基准。

Result: 在ToolBench上评估五种参数化模型配置，发现模糊查询下性能较完整查询基准下降50-64个百分点，低于嵌入基线；部分模型检索强但事实探测近乎随机。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索模型存在知识-检索分离现象，需要更全面的诊断框架来评估工具理解能力。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [145] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor是一个多智能体框架，通过结构化树搜索作为认知层，在大规模、有状态的动作空间中实现自主优化。它通过显式搜索树作为共享工作记忆，将失败视为诊断信号重塑探索，并在LLM推理优化中实现了高达193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，远超单一智能体的+33%且崩溃不可恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主优化系统只能处理孤立的、无状态评估的目标，无法应对需要跨多个技术栈（应用、框架、编译器、内核、硬件）协调优化的大型有状态系统，如全栈LLM推理优化。

Method: 提出Arbor多智能体框架，核心是结构化树搜索作为认知层，维护显式搜索树作为所有智能体的共享工作记忆；采用Orchestrator（驱动优化）与Critic（安全守护）的制衡架构；将智能体能力分解为硬技能（领域专长）和软技能（协调协议），实现完全自主的多日优化活动。

Result: 在LLM推理优化上实现高达193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，超过供应商优化基线；而单一智能体仅达到+33%吞吐量改进且数小时内崩溃不可恢复；方法跨多代硬件平台泛化，运行间方差在2个百分点以内，表明硬件无关且可重现。

Conclusion: Arbor通过结构化树搜索和多智能体制衡架构，成功将自主优化系统从孤立无状态场景扩展到大规模有状态系统，实现了显著优于单一智能体的性能提升，且具有良好的泛化性和可重现性。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation.
